In Bangladesh the Wage Earners' Welfare Board (the Welfare Board; Probashi Kallyan Bhaban, 71-72 Eskaton Garden, Ramna, Dhaka) pays grants and handles the insurance bought at BMET clearance, which Jiban Bima Corporation, the state life insurer, pays out \[[320](#o320), [321](#o321), [322](#o322), [N198](#n198)\].

### If you are injured or fall ill at work

1. **Get treatment.** Free at a PERKESO panel clinic (a private clinic PERKESO appoints) or a government clinic or hospital; after a workplace accident a panel clinic treats you free and checks your cover itself on PERKESO's SEHATi system \[[319](#o319), [323](#o323)\]. Panel clinics are listed by state (Selangor: perkeso.gov.my/images/Klinik_Panel/Senarai_Klinik/SENARAI_KLINIK_PANEL_SELANGOR.pdf); PERKESO's line is 1-300-22-8000 \[[324](#o324), [323](#o323)\]. Non-panel clinic costs can be claimed back from PERKESO \[[319](#o319)\]. At government hospitals SPIKPA covers you without a deposit (see Step 7 of the work visa), and sick leave must be reported within 48 hours (see Your rights at work) \[[142](#o142), [33 (2023)](#o33)\]. **Need to know:** a current list of PERKESO panel clinics for each state.
2. **Make sure the accident is reported and the claim filed.** The employer must report it to PERKESO at once, within 30 days at the latest, or face a fine or prosecution \[[325](#o325)\]. Covered: accidents at work, between home and work and during an emergency, work diseases, and since 1 June 2026 accidents outside work \[[319](#o319)\]. The claim is on Form 34-PA (perkeso.gov.my/images/borang/Borang34/290626-BORANG_34-PA.pdf); the worker or dependant signs as claimant, and the employer fills in its sections if you still work there \[[326](#o326), [327](#o327)\].
   - You'll need: Form 34-PA, passport and PLKS copies, your medical or sick-leave certificate, and for a road accident the original police report or a copy certified by the issuing police station \[[325](#o325)\]. Quote your 12-digit FWSS (Foreign Workers Social Security) number in every dealing with PERKESO \[[139 (2019)](#o139)\].
   - Where and when: hand it in at a PERKESO counter and keep the stamped slip, or post it to the nearest PERKESO office (the form asks which office you want to deal with); head office: Menara PERKESO, 281 Jalan Ampang, 50538 Kuala Lumpur \[[325](#o325), [327](#o327), [328](#o328)\]. File within 12 months of the benefit falling due (the appeal board can extend this for good reason); incomplete, unclear or late claims can be rejected \[[329 (2019)](#o329), [325](#o325)\].
   - Cost: free; don't use agents or middlemen who charge \[[325](#o325)\]. A false claim can bring up to 2 years' jail, a fine of up to RM10,000 or both \[[325](#o325)\]. Progress: 1 300 22 8000 or perkeso@perkeso.gov.my \[[325](#o325)\].
   - If PERKESO refuses or underpays, appeal to the Social Security Appellate Board within 3 years; a Medical Board's disability assessment goes to the Appellate Medical Board \[[329 (2019)](#o329)\].
   - **Need to know:** whether PERKESO accepts Form 34-PA without the employer's sections when the employer refuses to sign; the nearest PERKESO branch's address; how long PERKESO takes to decide; how to file an appeal to the Social Security Appellate Board.
3. **What you can get.** Temporary Disablement Benefit for medical leave of at least 4 days, counting the accident day \[[319](#o319)\]. For permanent disability, assessed by a Medical Board in Malaysia: up to 20% is paid as a lump sum; above 20%, one fifth can be taken as a lump sum and the rest monthly for life \[[319](#o319)\]. A 100% disabled worker needing constant care gets RM500 a month more, and PERKESO pays for physiotherapy, artificial limbs, wheelchairs and similar aids \[[319](#o319), [36](#o36)\]. A worker certified invalid who hasn't paid Invalidity Scheme contributions long enough gets a one-time Invalidity Grant of both sides' contributions with interest \[[319](#o319)\]. Payments can go to a bank outside Malaysia: give the account holder, bank, address, branch, account number and SWIFT code on the form, with a bank verification letter \[[327](#o327), [325](#o325)\].
4. **If the employer won't report it or pay you while you are off work,** file a case at the nearest Labour Department office (offices by state at jtksm.mohr.gov.my/ms/hubungi-kami/alamat-ibu-pejabat-dan-cawangan) with your payslips, contract and attendance record, and give a copy to the labour wing (counselorkl@probashi.gov.bd, +60 12-290 3252), which pursues compensation claims \[[330](#o330), [128 (2024)](#o128), [102](#o102), [N150](#n150)\]. The Labour Court works as in route 3 of "If you aren't paid"; if the employer ignores its Order, the Labour Department can seize and sell its property, take the money from its bank or prosecute it \[[91 (2017)](#o91)\]. Sample message to the labour wing: "I am [name], passport [number], PLKS with [employer, address]. I was injured at work on [date] at [place]. My employer has not reported it to PERKESO. Please help me claim." Tenaganita and the Bar Council's Legal Aid Centres help with cases (see route 4 of "If you aren't paid") \[[N143](#n143), [N154](#n154)\]; the ILO (International Labour Organization) does not take complaints from individual workers \[[331](#o331)\]. **Need to know:** whether Tenaganita charges workers for its help.
5. **Going home injured.** On your or your family's application, with proof of BMET clearance or Board membership and a passport copy, the Welfare Board asks the High Commission to bring you home \[[332](#o332)\]. The Board's ambulance at Dhaka and Chattogram airports is free on request \[[333](#o333)\].
6. **Treatment money in Bangladesh.** A returnee with BMET clearance or Board membership who is injured, sick or disabled can get up to Tk 2 lakh \[[334](#o334)\]. Within 1 year of return, the family applies to the Board's Director General via its web portal, the district probashi kallyan (migrant welfare) centre or the Board, with medical reports and prescriptions, a bank statement, a passport copy and a copy of its arrival stamp, proof of clearance or membership, and one 6R (6 by 8 inch) colour photo of the worker \[[334](#o334), [320](#o320)\]. A Board committee checks the case by WhatsApp video call and recommends an amount; free, up to 60 working days after all papers are in \[[334](#o334), [320](#o320)\].
7. **Insurance.** It pays Tk 10 lakh for total permanent disability and 10% to 90% of that for partial permanent disability \[[19](#o19)\]; see "Claiming the insurance".

### If a worker dies

These steps are for the family in Bangladesh.

1. **Ask the Welfare Board to bring the body home.** Call 16135 (from abroad +8809610102030) \[[79](#o79)\]. Then apply in one of these ways, even if the worker had no BMET clearance or Board membership \[[N199](#n199)\]:
   - on myGov (the government's online services portal), service "Application to bring home a migrant worker's body", https://www.mygov.bd/services/info?id=BDGS-1638167134, with a myGov account (register with your mobile number at idp-v2.live.mygov.bd/registration), the worker's passport and any clearance or membership; a tracking number comes by SMS and you can follow the case on myGov; free; up to 35 working days \[[335](#o335)\]; or
   - to the Ministry of Expatriates' Welfare, aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd, which also handles undocumented workers, describing what happened and the cause of death, with proof the person was a migrant worker (passport, work permit, BMET smart card, Malaysian ID, death certificate); free \[[95](#o95)\].
   - Sample: "My [relation], [name], passport [number], worked for [employer, phone] in [town], Malaysia, and died on [date]. We want the body brought home to [district]. The receiver is [name, phone]."
2. **Home or burial in Malaysia.** The Board passes the family's choice to the High Commission, and the family sends the High Commission a claim or consent letter naming the receiver and their phone number \[[336](#o336), [337](#o337), [128 (2024)](#o128)\]. For burial in Malaysia, apply on myGov with the service "Application for permission to bury a migrant worker's body in the destination country", https://www.mygov.bd/services/info?id=BDGS-1638246958, with the worker's passport or visa copy, any clearance or membership, the district office's investigation report, and a certificate signed by all heirs on the union parishad (rural local council) chairman's letterhead; free, 3 working days \[[338](#o338)\].
3. **The Malaysian side.** The employer writes to the High Commission, appoints a funeral company and sends its letters, the insurance claim and compensation letters, the hospital letter, the death certificate, the Malaysian police report and the passport \[[128 (2024)](#o128)\]. It pays the funeral and repatriation \[[37 (2022)](#o37)\]. For its no-objection certificate (NOC) the High Commission also needs a police report with NOC for an abnormal death (hanging, fall, accident, murder) and the employer's PERKESO death report; for an undocumented worker, the original passport (or a police report of its loss), the receiver's details, a police report with NOC, the original death certificate, the funeral company's letter and the employer's name and phone \[[339 (2023)](#o339)\]. The Board forwards the request within 3 to 5 working days, and the labour wing takes about 30 \[[335](#o335)\]. A documented worker's body usually comes home in reasonable time; an undocumented worker's can take much longer \[[N199](#n199)\].
   - If there is no employer or it refuses, the Welfare Board pays \[[121 (2024)](#o121), [128 (2024)](#o128)\]. PERKESO repays whoever paid (a person, company or the High Commission) up to RM4,500 of repatriation, even if the death isn't otherwise covered, on its PPJ (Funeral Repatriation Cost Claim) form, on which the employer states whether it paid, with a certified copy of the death certificate and of the claimant's ID or company registration, the original receipt, bank proof, and proof the body was sent home (the Ministry of Health's export permit or the airline's air waybill); hand in at a PERKESO counter or post \[[319](#o319), [340 (2022)](#o340), [341 (2020)](#o341)\].
   - Malaysian authorities trace the family through the permanent address and emergency contact in the worker's passport, so write a full name and a working phone number in the passport's emergency contact field; in 2026 a body stayed 15 days in a Kuala Lumpur morgue, at RM75 a day, because that field held only "0088" \[[311](#o311), [N200](#n200)\]. If no family is found, the worker may be buried in Malaysia as an unclaimed person \[[N200](#n200)\].
   - NGO and research officials say papers from the destination country usually give the cause as illness or natural death \[[N199](#n199)\]; Bangladesh doesn't check it independently, and migrants say deaths are recorded as stroke or heart attack to avoid workplace-accident compensation \[[N199](#n199), [N201](#n201)\]. If the family doubts the cause of death, apply to the Welfare Board for legal help abroad: myGov https://www.mygov.bd/services/info?id=BDGS-1638179893, at the Board, or through the expatriate welfare branch of the Deputy Commissioner's office (the district administration) or the district employment and manpower office (DEMO), with proof of clearance or membership and a passport or travel permit copy with arrival and departure stamps; free; sent to the labour wing within 1 to 2 days; up to 20 working days \[[342](#o342), [343](#o343)\]. **Need to know:** whether and how a family can ask the Malaysian police for a post-mortem or inquest.
4. **At the airport.** The Board's desk gives a Tk 35,000 cheque for transport and burial, collectable within 3 months, and a free ambulance \[[344](#o344), [333](#o333)\]. You'll need: the worker's passport copy; a heirship (warish) certificate from the union parishad chairman, mayor or city councillor; the NID of the wife, father or mother; the death certificate; the NOC; the original air waybill; proof of clearance or membership; and a photo and NID or passport copy of the named receiver, and of the collector if different \[[345](#o345), [344](#o344)\].
5. **The Tk 3 lakh Welfare Board grant,** for the family of a worker with clearance or membership who died in Malaysia, the country of the clearance \[[321](#o321)\]. If the family took the airport cheque, no application is needed: the Board writes asking for the papers; after burial in Malaysia, apply within 1 year of the death to the Board's Director General or through the district office \[[321](#o321)\]. The grant goes to the spouse, children and parents; for an unmarried worker with no living parents, to full siblings \[[321](#o321)\].
   - You'll need: a family-members certificate on the letterhead of the union parishad chairman, mayor or city councillor, countersigned by the UNO (upazila nirbahi officer, the sub-district's chief officer) or, in a city corporation, the regional executive officer; an indemnity, undertaking and authorisation on Tk 400 non-judicial stamp paper, countersigned the same way (samples on wewb.gov.bd); the NOC or death certificate copy; a bank certificate or original statement for the receiver's account with the routing number (minor children's own account where they apply); each family member's colour photo, attested by the chairman and the district welfare centre; and the worker's passport copy \[[321](#o321), [320](#o320)\].
   - Paid by bank transfer (BEFTN), with an SMS, within 60 working days of all papers reaching the Board \[[321](#o321), [320](#o320)\].
6. **The insurance:** Tk 10 lakh for a death within 5 years of the smart card date, for workers who left with clearance on or after 1 January 2023 \[[346](#o346), [19](#o19)\]. Cover for those who left between 19 December 2019 and 31 December 2022 lasted 2 years and has ended \[[N200](#n200)\]. See "Claiming the insurance". **Need to know:** whether those who left between 1 January and 11 March 2023 are covered, since BMET's leaflet gives the start as 12 March 2023 \[[19](#o19)\].
7. **Malaysian compensation.** PERKESO pays a Dependants' Benefit for a work-related death \[[319](#o319)\]; a funeral benefit against receipts of up to RM7,500 for burial in Bangladesh (up to RM4,500 to whoever paid repatriation, RM3,000 to the dependants) or up to RM3,000 for burial in Malaysia \[[319](#o319)\]; and, if the worker died in Malaysia before 60 from any cause, a Survivors' Pension of at least RM550 a month, if Invalidity Scheme contributions were paid for at least 24 of the 40 consecutive months before the claim or two-thirds of the months since they became due \[[319](#o319)\].
   - You'll need: Form 34-PA, the worker's passport and work pass copies, the death certificate or burial permit, proof of relationship (marriage certificate, widow's ID and children's birth certificates; or, with no wife or child, the worker's birth certificate and parents' IDs) and bank proof, with Bangladeshi papers translated into English by an accredited translator and certified by the High Commission or a Bangladesh authority; claim within 12 months \[[325](#o325), [329 (2019)](#o329)\]. For an accident, the Labour Department sends its Form H to the heirs, who fill it in and return it through the High Commission \[[128 (2024)](#o128)\].
   - How: when the High Commission reports that compensation may be due, the Welfare Board asks the family for a power of attorney and the papers, with free help from its legal cell, sends them to the High Commission and follows the case; the labour wing recovers compensation and unpaid wages, and the Board pays them to the heirs within 60 working days of having the papers and the money \[[347](#o347), [320](#o320), [121 (2024)](#o121)\]. The family can also ask the Ministry (aswelfare@probashi.gov.bd), which writes to the High Commission and the Board within 7 working days \[[95](#o95)\].
   - **Need to know:** how long PERKESO takes to pay a death claim filed through the High Commission.

### If you lose the job

1. **The employer must** tell the Labour Department within 30 days \[[33 (2023)](#o33)\]. Under the standard contract attached to the 2021 Bangladesh–Malaysia memorandum of understanding (MoU), it gives 2 months' notice or 2 months' wages, pays all money owed and pays your fare home unless you broke the contract \[[34 (2022)](#o34)\]. It sends you home with a Check Out Memo, Immigration's exit document for foreign workers \[[24](#o24)\]; with under 4 weeks' notice you may stay in its housing up to 4 weeks \[[146 (2021)](#o146)\]. You can't simply move to another employer; see "If you want to change employer" \[[43 (2024)](#o43)\].
2. **To claim money owed,** go to the Labour Court ("If you aren't paid") with your passport, the workplace's name and address, payslips and income details, appointment letter and terms of service, and any termination letter \[[91 (2017)](#o91)\].
3. **If you were dismissed without just cause,** file a written representation to the Director General of Industrial Relations, by hand at the Industrial Relations Department (JPP) office nearest the workplace, within 60 days of the dismissal or the end of the notice period, and keep the acknowledgement; union membership isn't needed and you can represent yourself \[[348 (2021)](#o348), [N202](#n202)\]. JPP tries to settle, then sends the case to the Industrial Court, which can award compensation only if you asked for reinstatement \[[348 (2021)](#o348), [N202](#n202)\]. A letter showing the case is pending usually gets you a Special Pass (see route 3 of "If you aren't paid") \[[N84 (2021)](#n84)\]. **Need to know:** the nearest JPP office's address, whether the form can be filed online, and any fee.
4. **Insurance:** coming home within 6 months of going after losing the job pays Tk 50,000 \[[19](#o19), [346](#o346)\]; see "Claiming the insurance".

### Claiming the insurance

1. **Call 16135** (+8809610102030 from abroad); the Welfare Board helps claim from Jiban Bima Corporation \[[79](#o79), [322](#o322)\].
2. **Apply.** For a death, nothing is needed if the body came through the airport desk; after burial in Malaysia or death after return, apply at portal.wewb.gov.bd or at the Board \[[320](#o320)\]. For disability or a lost job, apply to the Board's welfare section: Deputy Director, +88 02 48317085, 01321-158354, dd.welfare2@wewb.gov.bd (disability); Assistant Director, 01712-107301, jahanwewb@gmail.com (lost job) \[[320](#o320)\].
3. **You'll need** proof of clearance or membership and a passport copy \[[322](#o322)\], with departure and arrival stamps for a lost job \[[320](#o320)\]; for disability, medical papers and a 6R colour photo of the disabled part of the body \[[320](#o320)\]; for a death, the papers in step 5 of "If a worker dies". Free; 30 working days \[[320](#o320)\]. **Need to know:** how Jiban Bima Corporation pays the money (bank transfer or cheque).
4. **If it stalls** (families have waited months, with delays blamed on Jiban Bima Corporation and the Board \[[N198](#n198)\]), complain to the Board's grievance officer, +88 02 55138267, 01684-501891, azizwelfare@gmail.com (30 working days); then the Ministry's Joint Secretary (Administration), +88 02 41030261, jsadmin@probashi.gov.bd (20 working days); then grs.gov.bd, the Cabinet Division's grievance system \[[320](#o320)\]. The Ministry's complaint cell also takes email at minister.expat@gmail.com \[[194](#o194)\].

## Coming home

1. **Leaving Malaysia with cash.** You may carry any amount of foreign currency, and ringgit up to the equivalent of USD 10,000; above USD 10,000 in total, declare it on Customs form No. 22, which is available before the customs checkpoint, or face a fine of up to RM1 million, up to a year in jail or both \[[349](#o349), [350 (2009)](#o350)\].
2. **Customs in Bangladesh.** Duty-free: 65 kg of baggage, 2 used phones and 1 new phone a year, and once a year, if declared, 100 g of gold or 200 g of silver jewellery (no more than 12 pieces of one type) \[[351](#o351)\]. Once a year you may bring a declared gold bar up to 117 g (10 tola; duty Tk 5,000 per tola) or silver bar up to 234 g; a bigger or hidden bar is confiscated \[[351](#o351), [352](#o352)\]. No alcohol on a Bangladeshi passport \[[351](#o351)\]. Declare foreign currency over USD 10,000 on the FMJ form \[[352](#o352)\]. Customs emergency line: +88 01319-056591 \[[352](#o352)\]. **Need to know:** whether BMET card holders who have been abroad at least 6 months may bring 2 new phones a year duty-free.
3. **Airport help.** The Welfare Board's desks help with boarding cards, customs and lost luggage, take complaints of abuse or cheating, and send sick returnees to hospital by ambulance; Dhaka desk +8802 8901040, 01404 413388 \[[345](#o345), [333](#o333)\]. Women returning within a year after abuse or fraud get Tk 5,000 cash \[[1](#o1)\].
4. **A place to stay.** The Wage Earners' Centre (Lonjonipara, Borua, Khilkhet, Dhaka-1229, near Shahjalal airport): Tk 100 admission, Tk 200 a night; book at wec.wewb.gov.bd or in person with proof of clearance or membership, a passport copy and the ticket; 01310 350555, 01754 715720 (24 hours) \[[353](#o353)\]. The Probashi Lounge at Shahjalal airport gives free wifi and phone calls and food at up to 30% off when you show proof of clearance or membership \[[354](#o354)\].
5. **If your family is threatened or its property seized,** apply to the Welfare Board for legal help (myGov https://www.mygov.bd/services/info?id=BDGS-1638181581, the Board, the expatriate welfare branch of the Deputy Commissioner's office or the DEMO) with the worker's passport copy and any clearance or membership; free, up to 3 working days; the Board writes to the local administration, such as the Deputy Commissioner or Superintendent of Police \[[355](#o355), [356](#o356), [121 (2024)](#o121)\].
6. **A business loan.** Probashi Kallyan Bank (PKB, the government bank for migrants) lends returnees who start a project up to Tk 50 lakh, up to Tk 3 lakh without collateral, at 9% simple interest over up to 10 years; apply at the PKB branch for the business's area on its free form \[[357](#o357)\].
   - You'll need: 3 passport photos; passport and NID copies; a certificate of your present and permanent address from the pourashava (municipality) or union parishad; a current trade licence; project details with 2 years' income and expenses; the premises' lease or ownership papers; a declaration of your own investment and other loans; copies of your return papers; 3 signed cheque leaves from the business account; and a guarantor (a relative or anyone able to repay) with 2 photos, ID copy, address certificate, a clean CIB report (Bangladesh Bank's credit report showing no unpaid loans) and 3 signed cheque leaves \[[357](#o357)\].
   - Fees: 0.50% processing and 0.50% documentation, each capped at Tk 15,000, each plus VAT, paid when you take the loan; no service charge \[[357](#o357)\]. Decision within 15 working days of a complete application \[[357](#o357)\]. Helpline 16238, or +880 9677-787878 from abroad \[[32](#o32)\].
7. **BRAC's help.** BRAC's Migration Programme gives returnees and trafficking survivors airport help at Dhaka through its Migration Welfare Centre, counselling, money or materials to start earning, and legal help \[[358](#o358), [N203](#n203)\]. **Need to know:** how a returnee contacts it.
8. **Children's scholarships.** The Welfare Board pays migrants' children scholarships (Tk 27,500 a year at SSC level, Tk 34,000 at HSC) and reserves 0.5% of HSC admission seats for them \[[121 (2024)](#o121)\]. **Need to know:** how to apply.
